Model before rewriting:

find s: set (size 2) of int(1..2)

such that

|s| in s

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])

new variables:
  find s#packed_1: int(0..3)
new constraints:
  sum([(s#packed_1 / 1) % 2,(s#packed_1 / 2) % 2;int(1..)]) = 2

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
